> **`sonnet-south-asia-1` — South Asian Religious Modernity — finished 2026-05-16.** The most glaring missing cluster: Ambedkar, Iqbal, Gandhi entirely absent from vault. All three used religion as anti-colonial tool in British India; all three completely absent from standard comparative religion curricula. **Ambedkar** (*The Buddha and His Dhamma*, 1956): stripped karma as casteist ideology; nirvana = social liberation; [[event-ambedkar-mass-conversion-1956]] Nagpur Oct 14 1956 = largest single-day mass religious conversion in history (~500k Dalits). MASSIVE WIN: Navayana ↔ Liberation Theology ↔ Donghak ↔ Cao Dai — all four use religious doctrine as explicit anti-oppression program, independent parallel across four continents. **Iqbal** (*Reconstruction of Religious Thought in Islam*, 1930): Bergson + Nietzsche + tawhid — most systematic Islamic modernist text of 20th c. *Khudi* vs. *ātman=Brahman* = philosophical bedrock of Hindu-Muslim split → Pakistan. MASSIVE WIN: ijtihad-reopening → Fazlur Rahman → reformism; same call provoked Mawdudi's opposite response → Islamism. **Gandhi**: satyagraha = Jain ahimsa + Thoreau + Tolstoy + Sermon on the Mount; templates MLK, Mandela, Chavez, Dalai Lama. Gandhi vs. Ambedkar = unresolved founding debate of Indian democracy. New: tradition-navayana-buddhism, tradition-islamic-modernism, tradition-hinduism (hub, fixed 8 dead refs), tradition-sunni-islam (fixed 3 dead refs), event-ambedkar-mass-conversion-1956, liberation-theology theme. Scripture view: south-asian-modernism corpus. **2542 nodes · 15332 edges · 2.5% dead-link.**

> **`opus-pantheon-v2-rim-horiz-1` — Phase F · horizontal family rim labels + tick lines — finished 2026-05-17.** Replaced V2's tangential (rotated-along-rim) family labels with production-style horizontal text at `Router+56`, plus radial tick lines from inside the hull rim out to each label. **buildRimLabels** ([pantheon-v2.js:1048](src/js/views/pantheon-v2.js#L1048)) drops `rotate(rotDeg)`; each label now anchored via CSS percentages computed from the wedge-center angle — `cos > 0.35` → `tx=0%` (label extends right from anchor), `cos < -0.35` → `tx=-100%`, else `-50%`. Same for `sin > 0.55 / -0.55 / else` → `ty = -100% / 0% / -50%`. This is the DOM-percentage equivalent of production's `text-anchor: start|end|middle` + `dy: 0em|0.85em|0.35em` ([app.js:1190-1201](src/js/app.js#L1190)). Font size scales with wedge arc: `max(9, min(14, 8 + arc × 11))`. Big families (≥6 members) get `.ph2-rim-label-bright` for slightly brighter color. **Tick lines**: new SVG group `ticksG` inside the overlay, gets the Y-flipped camera transform from `syncOverlay()`. One `<line>` per wedge, from `(Router+6)` to `(Router+38)` along the wedge center angle, stroked at family color opacity 0.45 — geometry exactly mirrors production app.js:1169-1177. CSS adds halo text-shadow on `.ph2-rim-label` so labels read over hulls. Cache-bust `20260517-rim-horizontal`. Production untouched. **V2 is now visually at parity with production** on every front audited in the divergence-7 report (A edge discipline · B row radii · C node size · D force-bake · E DOM labels · F rim labels) plus the y-flip fix.

Eliade documented 40-day initiatory seclusion across Siberian/Central Asian/American shamanic traditions. (3) **Sacred-108 astronomical grounding MASSIVE WIN** — Earth-Sun distance ≈ 108 × Sun's diameter; Earth-Moon distance ≈ 108 × Moon's diameter; Sun's diameter ≈ 108 × Earth's diameter — three convergent ratios known to classical Indian astronomers. Arithmetic: 108 = 4 × 27 nakshatras = 12 × 9 navamsas. Hindu: 108-bead mala / 108 names of deities / 108 Upanishads (Muktikopanishad list) / 108 Divya Desams / 108 Shaktipithas / 108 Nataraja karanas / 108 Marma points. (4) **Prayer-bead migration MASSIVE WIN** — Hindu mala (108 beads) → Buddhist mala (108, shared count) → Catholic rosary (150 beads, same technology) → Islamic misbaha/tasbih (99 beads = 99 names of Allah); Thurston 1907 (Tier-1 Jesuit scholarship) documents Eastern antecedents of the Catholic rosary. Chinese 12-animal zodiac parallel (years not months; animal units not constellations) — structural convergence whether independent or transmitted. (6) **Nsibidi / Ekpe MASSIVE WIN** — Ejagham ideographic script (Cross River, Nigeria, c. 400–1400 CE archaeological record) is the institutional script of the Ekpe (Ngbe) leopard graded secret society — simultaneously legal/judicial/governmental/cosmological; grade-restricted sign access = initiatic knowledge hierarchy. Single most striking fact: the **Abakuá society** (Havana, 1836), founded by enslaved Calabar-region Ekpe members, preserved nsibidi-derived signs in Cuban ritual — **the only documented case of a specific African secret-society writing system surviving intact through the Middle Passage**. Thompson 1983 *Flash of the Spirit* (Tier-1) + Sosa Rodríguez 1982 *Los Ñañigos* (Tier-1, Havana). Structural parallel to Freemasonic cipher alphabets / Rosicrucian secret scripts as grade-restricted initiatic graphic systems — documented as independent convergence. > **`opus-pantheon-v2-yflip-1` — CRITICAL · SVG overlay Y-flip fix — finished 2026-05-17.** Phase E exposed a structural bug present since the original V2 commit but invisible until DOM labels made each node identifiable: **sigma uses math-convention world Y (positive Y = up), SVG uses canvas convention (positive Y = down), and `syncOverlay()` was computing scale as `Math.hypot(u.x-o.x, u.y-o.y)` and applying it as a single uniform value to both axes**. Result: every hull and curved edge in the SVG overlay painted MIRRORED across the horizontal axis from the sigma-rendered nodes. Egyptian nodes upper-right at viewport y≈300, Egyptian hull at y≈600. Invisible while nodes were clustered on a thin inner ring (the wide annular hull + mirrored band still overlapped), obvious as soon as Phase D spread the nodes radially and Phase E pinned each label to its actual node. **Fix** ([pantheon-v2.js:657](src/js/views/pantheon-v2.js#L657)): probe both axes — `ux = graphToViewport(1,0)`, `uy = graphToViewport(0,1)` — recover `sx = ux.x - o.x` and `sy = uy.y - o.y` separately, then `transform = translate(o.x o.y) scale(${sx} ${sy})`. `sy` comes back negative (~−0.93 at camera ratio 0.78), which flips SVG Y to match sigma's world Y. Verified live: Egyptian hull page-rect (858–1116, 301–427) now contains Egyptian-node average page position (1002, 367). Cache-bust `20260517-yflip-fix`.

> **`opus-pantheon-v2-labels-1` — Phase E · DOM node labels + deconfliction — finished 2026-05-17.** Sigma's built-in labels paint at node-center and de-overlap via a grid that just *hides* collisions — why labels piled up on the inner ring in every prior V2 screenshot. Replaced with production's strategy ([app.js:1252-1319](src/js/app.js#L1252), `deconflictNodeLabels()` at [1444](src/js/app.js#L1444)): SVG-text-in-a-layer → ported as DOM-overlay-with-divs (one per deity, ~487 total). Each label positioned ABOVE its node via CSS `transform: translate(-50%, -100%)` plus computed `dy = 7 + √deg × 1.5`. Halo is a 4-corner text-shadow stack mimicking production's `paint-order:stroke + stroke=bg-0`. Two-line stacking for "Enki / Ea" double-names. **Deconfliction**: greedy first-fit by degree — measure visible labels' bounding rects, sort by deg desc, walk claiming screen-space; conflicts → `visibility: hidden`. Runs 60 ms after every camera sync. **Result**: ~87 labels visible at default zoom (vs 304 piled on the inner ring before). Sigma labels fully suppressed in nodeReducer (`out.label = ''`). Wiring: hover dim via `applyLabelHoverDim()`, `_labelsMode` toggle calls `updateNodeLabelVisibility()`, family legend click also calls it. Dev panel's `hubThreshold` slider still controls threshold live. Cache-bust `20260517-dom-labels`. Production Pantheon untouched.

> **`goblin-islamic-sacred-1` — Islamic Sacred Symbols + Sudarshana + Ashtamangala — 4 new symbol nodes — finished 2026-05-16.** 4 new `status: full` symbol nodes in `09_symbols/`. **NEW: `rub-el-hizb.md`** — The Islamic 8-pointed star / Quranic typographic mark (formed by two overlapping squares at 45°). MASSIVE WIN 1: **8-pointed star cross-tradition convergence** — Star of Ishtar (Mesopotamia -2350 BCE) / Rub el Hizb (Islamic 650 CE) / Christian octagram (8th day = resurrection) / Star of Lakshmi (Hindu *ashtakona*) — four independent traditions converging on the 8-pointed star through their own internal logic (Venus cycle / Quranic division / resurrection numerology / yantra geometry). MASSIVE WIN 2: **Quranic navigation system** — the Rub el Hizb mark appears 240 times in a complete Quran, marking the end of each *rub' al-hizb* (quarter-section of the 60-*hizb* division system); one of the oldest forms of sacred typographic navigation. MASSIVE WIN 3: **Girih bridge** — the Rub el Hizb geometry IS the 8-pointed star tile unit of the *girih* tile system (the quasi-periodic Islamic geometric ornament); textual mark and architectural geometry are the same form in different registers. Cross-symbol edges: star-of-ishtar (parallel-form), crescent-moon-star (visual-cognate), arabesque-girih (component-of), dharmachakra (parallel-form — both 8-based sacred geometric forms). 4 T1 refs (Bloom & Blair 1997, Nasr 1987, Denny 2004, Lu & Steinhardt 2007). **NEW: `basmala.md`** — *Bismi-llāhi r-raḥmāni r-raḥīm* as sacred calligraphic image — the Islamic answer to the icon. MASSIVE WIN 1: **Sacred-word-as-visual-image cross-tradition group completed** — Basmala (Islamic calligraphy) / Tetragrammaton (Jewish written divine name) / Aum/Om (Hindu visual glyph) / IHS Chi-Rho (Christian Christogram): four traditions making the same move — the divine name as a written form with independent sacred power as image. MASSIVE WIN 2: **Sufi letter-mysticism = Kabbalistic letter-mysticism** — Ibn ʿArabī's fractal doctrine (Quran → Basmala → *bāʾ* → dot beneath *bāʾ*) is structurally identical to the Kabbalistic doctrine (Torah → *Bereishit* → *bet* → dot at its corner): both read the smallest unit of sacred writing as a cosmological plenum. MASSIVE WIN 3: **99 Divine Names** — the Basmala encodes two of the 99 *asmāʾ Allāh al-ḥusnā* (*ar-Raḥmān* + *ar-Raḥīm*); the 99-Names system parallels the Kabbalistic 72 Names and the Hindu sahasranāma as comprehensive divine-attribute vocabularies. Cross-symbol edges: aum-om (parallel-form), tetragrammaton (parallel-form), arabesque-girih (co-appears-with). 4 T1 refs (Nasr 1987, Blair 2006, Schimmel 1984, Bloom & Blair 1997). **NEW: `sudarshana-chakra.md`** — Vishnu's spinning discus-weapon (*su-darśana cakra*) — the most important of the four Vaishnava divine attributes. MASSIVE WIN 1: **PIE returning divine weapon pattern** — Sudarshana Chakra / Mjolnir / Vajra / Perun's axe: the thunder-god's returning weapon that destroys chaos and returns to the defender; one of the most robustly documented Proto-Indo-European divine weapon patterns. MASSIVE WIN 2: **Wheel of Ezekiel parallel** — Ezekiel's *ophanim* (spinning wheels of fire in the Merkavah vision, Ezekiel 1) are the Biblical-Jewish structural parallel to the Sudarshana: spinning cosmic wheels of divine fire encoding divine order; independent Indic and Semitic articulations of the same intuition. MASSIVE WIN 3: **Sudarshana Yantra = Vaishnava Sri Yantra** — the Sudarshana Yantra occupies the same structural position in South Indian Vaishnavism that the Sri Yantra occupies in Shaktism: the supreme geometric encoding of a divine principle. MASSIVE WIN 4: **Dharmachakra polarity** — weapon-form vs. law-form of the cosmic spinning wheel: Sudarshana (destruction of adharma) / Dharmachakra (the path of dharma): same Indic *cakra* cosmological vocabulary, radically different registers. Cross-symbol edges: dharmachakra (parallel-form), shankha-conch (co-appears-with), sri-yantra (parallel-form), mjolnir (parallel-form), vajra-dorje (parallel-form). 4 T1 refs (Gopinatha Rao 1914, Daniélou 1964, Mallory & Adams 2006). **NEW: `ashtamangala.md`** — The Eight Buddhist Auspicious Symbols as a closed iconographic system (Dharmachakra + Lotus + Endless Knot + Treasure Vase + Parasol + Golden Fish + Conch + Victory Banner). MASSIVE WIN 1: **Royal iconography transfer** — the eight symbols originate in early Indian royal-welcome iconography (*chakravartin* reception); Buddhism re-semanticizes each: the parasol of sovereignty → divine protection; the wheel of empire → Dharmachakra; the fish of abundance → liberation from *saṃsāra* — one of the clearest cases of the pan-tradition pattern of spiritualizing royal visual vocabulary. MASSIVE WIN 2: **Golden fish + Christian Ichthys = complete independent convergence** — the *suvarṇamatsya* (Buddhist golden fish, -270 BCE, India) and the ICHTHYS symbol (Christian fish, 1st–2nd c. CE, Levant) are both fish as primary sacred emblems, developed entirely independently; same animal-form, entirely different semantic content, same status as primary emblem: strongest independent convergence in sacred iconography. MASSIVE WIN 3: **Closed symbol-set cross-tradition pattern** — Ashtamangala (8 symbols for complete auspiciousness) / Noble Eightfold Path (8 steps for complete liberation) / Shinto Three Imperial Regalia (3 symbols for complete authority) / Seven Sacraments (7 symbols for complete grace): the use of a defined closed set to map total sacred reality is a cross-tradition pattern in its own right. Cross-symbol edges: dharmachakra (contains), lotus (contains), endless-knot (contains), shankha-conch (contains), fish-ichthys (parallel-form). 4 T1 refs (Beer 1999, Huntington 1985, Rhie & Thurman 1991, Metzger 1987). **Build: 153 symbols · 14326 edges · 0 YAML errors · 0 new dead links introduced.**

> **`opus-pantheon-v2-bake-1` — Phase D · force-relaxation bake + camera fit — finished 2026-05-17.** Side-by-side with production exposed two more root issues after A+B+C: (1) within each row, nodes packed tangentially with zero breathing room — production's `d3.forceSimulation` gives every node space to nudge sideways, V2 had nothing; (2) sigma's default camera at ratio 1.0 leaves ~25% padding so the diagram looked sparse. **Phase D landed both**: `relaxPositions()` is a one-shot pre-paint force pass (~150 iterations, ~50 ms for 500 deities) — three forces (anchor toward computed wedge slot, inverse-square charge repulsion within wedge, positional collide ∝ √degree) + hard wedge clamp every iteration (radial Rinner+14 → Router−14, angular ±halfArc with padA). Critical perf decision: pairwise charge limited to nodes inside the same wedge (O(Σ wedge²) ≈ 10k, not O(N²) = 250k). Bake runs once between `computeWedgePositions` and `graph.addNode`; sigma never sees a live simulation, zero ongoing render cost. **Camera**: `sigma.getCamera().setState({ ratio: 0.78 })` post-init zooms the world to fill viewport like production. **Visual delta vs A+B+C**: rows now plainly visible as three concentric arcs per big family; hubs (Shiva, YHWH, Marduk, Mary) push siblings sideways for real breathing room. Remaining gaps (node-label clustering, tangential family rim labels) queued for E + F. Cache-bust `20260517-force-bake`. Production Pantheon untouched.

> **`goblin-dragon-1` — Dragon East/West + Stupa + Thunderbird symbol thread — 4 new symbol nodes — finished 2026-05-16.** 4 new `status: metadata` symbol nodes in `09_symbols/`. **NEW: `dragon-eastern.md`** — The Chinese lóng (龍) and East Asian dragon tradition; period-earliest -1600 (Shang dynasty bronze vessels). **MASSIVE WIN 1: The great symbol-polarity** — Eastern dragon (benevolent, rain-bringing, imperial) vs. Western dragon (malevolent, adversarial, Satanic) share the same morphological type but carry diametrically opposite valences — one of the most striking cross-tradition symbol-polarity cases in comparative religion. **MASSIVE WIN 2: Imperial dragon theology** — Son of Heaven (zhēn lóng, 真龍) embodies the dragon's cosmic power; five-clawed = emperor, four = nobility, three = commons; dragon heraldry IS Chinese political theology. **MASSIVE WIN 3: Dragon Kings and rain** — Lóng Wáng (龍王) govern the four seas; farmers pray to them for rain; Chinese dragon is primarily a water deity — polar opposite of European dragon's treasure-hoarding function. **MASSIVE WIN 4: Yamata-no-Orochi exception** — the 8-headed serpent monster of the Kojiki shows that even within East Asia the serpent is not uniformly benevolent. Cross-symbol edges: dragon-western (polemic-inversion), naga-serpent (parallel-form — serpent-as-cosmic-water-power complex), feathered-serpent (parallel-form — winged lóng = structural parallel to Quetzalcoatl). 4 refs (Visser 1913 T1, Rawson 1992 T1, Christie 1968 T2, Cooper 1978 T2). **NEW: `dragon-western.md`** — The Western dragon from Enuma Elish to Revelation, covering Leviathan/Lotan, the PIE dragon-slaying pattern, and hagiographic beast. **MASSIVE WIN 1: Proto-Indo-European dragon-slaying pattern** — West 2007 documents the single PIE myth underlying Indra-Vrtra / Marduk-Tiamat / Baal-Lôtan/Yahweh-Leviathan / Zeus-Typhon / Thor-Jörmungandr / George-dragon. **MASSIVE WIN 2: Leviathan-Lotan cognate** — Hebrew lwytn = Ugaritic ltn (Cassuto 1971, Day 1985 T1): the best-documented case of direct mythological transmission in the Hebrew Bible; Baal Cycle → Isaiah 27:1 / Psalm 74:14 / Job 41. **MASSIVE WIN 3: Revelation dragon = Satan identification** — Rev 12:9 explicitly names the dragon = ancient serpent = Devil = Satan; permanent Christianization of the dragon symbol as cosmic adversary. **MASSIVE WIN 4: Polarity with Eastern dragon** — structural cross-symbol polemic-inversion edge to dragon-eastern. Cross-symbol edges: dragon-eastern (polemic-inversion), serpent-cosmic-enemy (ancestor-of), naga-serpent (parallel-form — Vrtra/nāga), ouroboros (syncretic-fusion — Leviathan as ouroboros in Kabbalistic tradition). 4 refs (West 2007 T1, Day 1985 T1, Cassuto 1971 T1, Visser 1913 T1). **NEW: `stupa.md`** — The Buddhist sacred mound as tomb / cosmic map / axis mundi; period-earliest -270 (Ashoka era). **MASSIVE WIN 1: Man-made sacred mountain cross-tradition pattern** — Egyptian pyramid (benben mound) / Mesopotamian ziggurat (Etemenanki) / Hindu temple shikhara / Mesoamerican pyramid-teocalli / Gothic cathedral nave = 5+ independent instantiations of the identical architectural-theological logic: sacred space = mountain = earth-heaven junction. **MASSIVE WIN 2: Aṇḍa as cosmic egg** — the dome IS the brahmāṇḍa; relic = seed; stupa = womb; creation-through-cosmic-egg theology encoded in Buddhist monumental architecture. **MASSIVE WIN 3: Ka'ba circumambulation parallel** — Buddhist pradakṣiṇā and Islamic tawāf are structurally identical centering-circumambulatory rituals at independent sacred monuments; no transmission. **MASSIVE WIN 4: Borobudur** — world's largest Buddhist monument; 9-terraced mandala-pyramid; walking its circuits = ascending through all Buddhist cosmological realms. Cross-symbol edges: axis-mundi (syncretic-fusion), cosmic-egg (syncretic-fusion — aṇḍa dome), mandala-circle (syncretic-fusion), pyramid-triangle (parallel-form — man-made-sacred-mountain), dharmachakra (syncretic-fusion — harmika-wheel fusion). 4 refs (Snodgrass 1985 T1, Dehejia 1972 T1, Harvey 1990 T1, Dallapiccola 1980 T1). **NEW: `thunderbird.md`** — The storm-bird of Pacific Northwest, Plains, and Algonquin Native American traditions; period-earliest -500 (petroglyphs). **MASSIVE WIN 1: Universal storm-bird pattern** — Native American Thunderbird / Akkadian Anzu-Imdugud (c.-2600) / Vedic Garuda / Norse Hræsvelgr = four+ independent traditions on the giant bird as storm-power generator; functional constants across all instances (giant bird, storm-sky association, weather generated by body motion). **MASSIVE WIN 2: Eagle-vs.-serpent polarity** — Thunderbird vs. Mishepeshu (Ojibwe Great Horned Underwater Serpent) = Native American instantiation of Garuda vs. Nāga / eagle vs. serpent / Indra vs. Vrtra / Zeus/Apollo vs. Python: one of the most globally distributed symbolic dyads in world religion. **MASSIVE WIN 3: Wakinyan and the heyoka** — Lakota Thunderbird vision creates obligation of ritual contrary (heyoka), integrating storm-power into the medicine wheel's West direction; Black Elk's testimony documented. **MASSIVE WIN 4: Lévi-Strauss structural analysis** — *Way of the Masks* (1982 T1) analyzes Northwest Coast Thunderbird as a structural operator in transformation sets across the mask corpus. Cross-symbol edges: thunderbolt-keraunos (parallel-form — storm-power internalized vs. externalized), naga-serpent (parallel-form — eagle-vs.-serpent polarity), medicine-wheel (syncretic-fusion — Wakinyan assigned to West direction), feathered-serpent (parallel-form — bird+serpent fused vs. opposed). 4 refs (Lévi-Strauss 1982 T1, Hultkrantz 1957 T1, Vecsey 1983 T1, Smithsonian Handbook T1). **Build: 151 symbols · 14307 edges · 0 YAML errors · 0 new dead links introduced.**

> **`goblin-alchemic-script-1` — Alchemy + Writing Systems symbol thread — 4 new symbol nodes — finished 2026-05-16.** 4 new `status: metadata` symbol nodes in `09_symbols/`. **NEW: `azoth.md`** — The alchemical universal solvent / Mercury quintessence; Paracelsus (1493–1541) as primary theorist; Khunrath's *Amphitheatrum Sapientiae Aeternae* (1595) compass-rose diagram as canonical iconography. **MASSIVE WIN 1: Alphabetic totality symbol** — Azoth (A to Z / Alpha to Omega / Aleph to Tav) as the complete alphabet of nature; directly parallels Revelation 1:8 ("I am Alpha and Omega") and Sefer Yetzirah's 22-letter creation-cosmology — three traditions on the alphabet as cosmic completeness. **MASSIVE WIN 2: Solve et Coagula mechanics** — Azoth is the operational agent of the Magnum Opus: dissolves all substances to prima materia (solve) and enables the subsequent coagulation at higher form; the two nodes (azoth + solve-et-coagula) are procedurally inseparable. Cross-symbol edges: alpha-omega (parallel-form — alphabetic totality), prima-materia (syncretic-fusion), solve-et-coagula (syncretic-fusion), sulphur-mercury-salt (syncretic-fusion — Azoth = quintessential Mercury), rebis (parallel-form), ouroboros (parallel-form). 4 T1 refs (Principe 2013, Debus 1977, Read 1936, Khunrath 1595). **NEW: `athanor.md`** — The specialized alchemical furnace maintaining constant heat for the Magnum Opus; from Arabic *al-tannūr* (furnace, oven; cf. Hebrew *tannur* = Genesis 15:17 covenant firepot). **MASSIVE WIN 1: Body as athanor** — Paracelsian speculative alchemy: the practitioner's vital heat IS the athanor; the inner Magnum Opus performs the soul's transmutation. **MASSIVE WIN 2: Tibetan Tummo cross-tradition** — Vajrayana inner heat yoga (*gtum mo*): measurable physiological inner fire for spiritual transformation; body-as-athanor structural parallel with zero historical connection between Tibetan and Hermetic traditions — strongest independent-invention parallel in the vault. **MASSIVE WIN 3: Hesychast Divine Fire** — Gregory Palamas and the uncreated divine energies; theosis through the body-as-sacred-vessel; three independent traditions (Hermetic / Vajrayana / Eastern Orthodox) converging on the body as the vessel of inner transforming fire. **MASSIVE WIN 4: Divine smith cross-tradition** — Hephaestus / Vulcan / Lugh (Goibniu's forge) / Thor / dwarves' furnace as mythological ancestors of the athanor; mortal forge → alchemical athanor → inner spiritual furnace as one continuous metaphor. **MASSIVE WIN 5: Pottery kiln as cosmological ancestor** — Neolithic kilns (c. 6000 BCE) embody the identical cognitive structure: controlled heat + time = irreversible transformation of formless clay into permanent form. Cross-symbol edges: prima-materia (syncretic-fusion), philosophers-stone (syncretic-fusion), solve-et-coagula (syncretic-fusion), cosmic-egg (parallel-form), sacred-fire-atash (parallel-form). 4 T1 refs (Principe 2013, Holmyard 1957, Tylecote 1992, Read 1936). **NEW: `proto-sinaitic.md`** — The oldest attested alphabetic writing system, c. 1850–1550 BCE, from Semitic miners at Serabit el-Khadim turquoise mines, Sinai. **THE MOST MASSIVE WIN IN THE VAULT: Every letter you are reading right now is a direct descendant of a pictogram drawn by anonymous Semitic workers at an Egyptian turquoise mine nearly four thousand years ago.** The acrophonic principle (Aleph = ox, Beth = house, Gimel = camel): ~22 simplified pictograms whose names begin with the sound they represent, reducing Egyptian hieroglyphs to the simplest and most generative writing system ever devised. **MASSIVE WIN 1: Full global descent chain documented** — Proto-Sinaitic (c.1850-1550 BCE) → Phoenician → Aramaic → Hebrew/Arabic; Phoenician → Greek (with vowel invention) → Latin → Cyrillic; Aramaic → Brahmi → Devanagari + all South/Southeast Asian scripts — 5 billion people write in systems descending from these miners' marks. **MASSIVE WIN 2: Kabbalistic sacred-alphabet mysticism materially grounded** — Sefer Yetzirah's 22 letters as cosmic creation-forces descend materially from the Proto-Sinaitic pictographic system; the Kabbalistic weight of Aleph (silent primordial letter) is inseparable from its origin as an ox-head drawn at a mine. **MASSIVE WIN 3: Ogham parallel-form** — Both Proto-Sinaitic and Ogham (Irish tree-alphabet) are acrophonic-cosmological alphabets where each letter names a concrete thing in the world; independent convergence on the alphabet-as-cosmology form. mystery-status: partially-deciphered. Cross-symbol edges: alpha-omega (ancestor-of — documented Tier-1 transmission chain), sefirot-tree-of-life (parallel-form — Kabbalistic letter-mysticism operates on Proto-Sinaitic descendants), indus-valley-script (parallel-form — independent pictographic simplification). 4 T1 refs (Goldwasser 2010, Sass 1988, Robinson 2007, Albright 1966). **NEW: `rongorongo.md`** — Undeciphered hieroglyphic/logographic script from Rapa Nui (Easter Island); 26 surviving wooden tablets; one of only 3–4 independent writing-system inventions in human history; reverse boustrophedon reading direction (tablet rotated 180° at each line). **MASSIVE WIN 1: Most isolated script mystery in world** — Easter Island: 3,512 km from nearest continent; the people who could read the tablets were destroyed in the 1862 Peruvian slave raids (specifically targeting chiefs and learned men) and subsequent smallpox epidemic; 3,000–15,000 Rapa Nui reduced to 111 people by 1877; the fastest and most complete destruction of a writing tradition on record. **MASSIVE WIN 2: Independent invention debate** — if pre-contact invention (before 1770 Spanish contact), most recent case of genuinely independent writing-system invention; if post-contact stimulus diffusion, most remarkable case of rapid spontaneous script creation. **MASSIVE WIN 3: Fischer decipherment documented as contested** — his 1997 procreation-chant reading is Tier-1 for corpus/sign-catalogue work but Tier-3 for decipherment claims (Sproat 2003 information-theoretic critique documented); the discipline of tiering contested decipherments maintained throughout. mystery-status: mystery. Cross-symbol edges: phaistos-disc (parallel-form — both undeciphered mystery scripts from isolated island contexts), indus-valley-script (parallel-form). 4 T1 refs (Fischer 1997, Barthel 1958, Métraux 1940, Horley 2010). **Build: 143 symbols · 14216 edges · 0 YAML errors · 0 new dead links introduced.**

> **`goblin-nature-fertility-1` — Nature + Fertility symbol thread — 4 new symbol nodes — finished 2026-05-16.** 4 new `status: metadata` symbol nodes in `09_symbols/`. **NEW: `green-man.md`** — The foliate head of medieval Christian church architecture; term coined by Lady Raglan 1939 (*Folklore*). **MASSIVE WIN 1: Roman-transmission hypothesis (Basford 1978 T1)** — the Green Man is NOT documented as a Celtic pagan survival; the acanthus-disgorging-mask of Roman temple friezes (c. 1st c. BCE onward) is the direct architectural ancestor, transmitted via Roman stonecutters into early medieval church building; the pagan-survival claim (Raglan 1939 Tier 3) is definitively unsupported by evidence (Hutton 1999 T1). **MASSIVE WIN 2: Three independent vegetation-face parallels** — (1) Mesopotamian Huwawa/Humbaba (*Epic of Gilgamesh*, c. -2100 BCE) — forest giant whose face IS entrails/vegetation; oldest foliate-face figure in world literature; (2) Dionysus/Bacchus as vine-covered mask-god; (3) Osiris as the god whose body = vegetation. Three independent attestations of the deity-as-vegetation-face before the medieval Christian form; none documented as transmissions. **MASSIVE WIN 3: Green Man + Sheela-na-Gig pairing** — both appear on the same Romanesque churches at the same architectural level flanking doorways; the pair constitutes a complete cosmological sexual polarity (male vegetative force + female genital force = full generative power defending the sacred threshold); documented at Kilpeck Church, Herefordshire (c. 1140 CE). 6 refs including Basford 1978 T1, Anderson 1990 T1, Hutton 1999 T1, Weir & Jerman 1986 T1, Andersen 1977 T1. **NEW: `sheela-na-gig.md`** — The naked female figure displaying exaggerated genitalia on Romanesque and medieval Irish/British church exteriors; c. 110 Irish + 45 British examples documented (McMahon & Roberts 2001 T1). **MASSIVE WIN 1: Theological paradox** — fertility/sexuality figures on Christian sacred buildings, no written medieval explanation; three scholarly hypotheses (apotropaic / memento mori / pagan survival) documented and tiered. **MASSIVE WIN 2: 25,000-year sacred-vulva vocabulary** — Paleolithic Venus figurines (-25,000 BCE) → Çatalhöyük figurines (-7500 BCE) → Minoan figures → medieval Sheela; the Sheela as a Christianized continuation of the most ancient documented visual vocabulary in world art; plus Hindu yoni and Roman Baubo as independent parallel attestations. Mystery-status: contested. 4 refs: Andersen 1977 T1, Weir & Jerman 1986 T1, McMahon & Roberts 2001 T1, Hutton 1991 T1. **NEW: `shiva-lingam.md`** — The primary sacred image of Śiva; upright cylindrical stone shaft in yoni base; most numerous sacred image in South and Southeast Asian religion; Indus Valley proto-lingams c. -2600 BCE. **MASSIVE WIN 1: Jyotirlinga as axis mundi** — the *Śiva Purāṇa* describes the cosmic lingam as an infinite pillar of light through all cosmic levels; Viṣṇu descends to find its base, Brahmā ascends to find its summit, neither succeeds — the lingam IS the axis mundi. **MASSIVE WIN 2: Four independent phallic-axis attestations** — Egyptian Min (erected phallus as divine creative power, c. -3100 BCE); Egyptian Osiris golden replacement phallus (pivot of resurrection mystery); Greek herms (phallic upright shaft as boundary marker, road marker, cosmic axis); Canaanite maṣṣēbôt (standing stones as marks of divine presence) — four independent civilizations converging on the upright-stone as axis/boundary/cosmic power. **MASSIVE WIN 3: Sāṃkhya cosmology encoded** — Lingam (Puruṣa / static consciousness) + Yoni (Prakṛti / dynamic creative nature) = manifest cosmos; the entire Sāṃkhya ontology compressed into one sculptural image. 5 refs: Kramrisch 1981 T1, Doniger O'Flaherty 1973 T1, Rao 1914 T1, Kinsley 1988 T1, Śiva Purāṇa T1. **NEW: `yoni.md`** — The cosmic female creative principle in Hindu and Tantric tradition; Sanskrit *yonī* = womb, source, origin; the base of the Shiva lingam; the sacred aperture at Kāmākhyā temple whose inner sanctum has no image (only a rock cleft through which a stream flows — the absent image IS the image). **MASSIVE WIN 1: Kāmākhyā's absent image** — the most sacred yoni-pitha has no representation; the formless creative source cannot be imaged; the Ambubachi Mela (annual "menstruation" of the goddess) — Śakti's fertility at peak; tradition documented in Kinsley 1988 T1. **MASSIVE WIN 2: 25,000-year deep-time parallel** — Venus of Willendorf (-25,000 BCE) → Çatalhöyük (-7500 BCE) → Minoan → Isis/Nut → Aphrodite → Tantric Yoni-Pitha → Sheela-na-Gig: the longest continuous (parallel-form) tradition of sacred female generative power in world religion. **MASSIVE WIN 3: Mandorla = yoni (Gothic architecture)** — Simson 1956 (*The Gothic Cathedral*, Princeton UP, T1) documents the Gothic pointed arch and Christ Pantokrator mandorla as geometrically identical to the yoni-triangle; the sacred opening through which divinity enters the world is the same pointed-oval form in Hindu and Gothic architectural traditions; no documented transmission — parallel-form. **MASSIVE WIN 4: Cosmic womb cross-tradition** — Hebrew Rūaḥ (feminine grammatical gender, moving over the primordial waters), Tiamat (Mesopotamian mother-ocean), Nut (Egyptian sky-womb), Gaia (Greek self-generating earth), Prakriti (Hindu undifferentiated creative nature) — the undifferentiated primordial creative void has a persistent feminine face across five independent traditions. 5 refs: Kinsley 1988 T1, Kramrisch 1981 T1, Simson 1956 T1, Doniger O'Flaherty 1973 T1, Flood 2006 T1. **Build: 141 symbols · 14193 edges · 0 YAML errors · 0 new dead links introduced.**

> **`goblin-sacred-sound-1` — Sacred Sound instruments symbol thread — 4 new symbol nodes — finished 2026-05-16.** 4 new `status: metadata` symbol nodes in `09_symbols/`. **NEW: `sistrum.md`** — The Egyptian sacred rattle (*sššt*); primary instrument of Hathor and Isis cults; the sound IS divine presence, not merely accompaniment. Two form variants: sekhem (rectangular closed-loop, Old/Middle Kingdom, apotropaic) and naos (shrine-shaped top, Middle Kingdom onward, Roman Isis cult canonical form). **MASSIVE WIN 1: Roman Isis cult transmission** — most thoroughly documented case of Egyptian cult-object export in antiquity; Pompeii bronze sistrum + Apuleius *Metamorphoses* Book XI (c. 160 CE) as primary source for the sistrum in Roman Isis procession; the protagonist Lucius hears the sistrum as first signal of divine rescue — one of the richest ancient accounts of sacred-sound theology. `phase-4-105-apuleius-metamorphoses-book-11` + `phase-4-072-plutarch-de-iside-et-osiride` wired. **MASSIVE WIN 2: Plutarch's cosmological interpretation** — *De Iside et Osiride* §63–64: four bars = four elements; curved top = lunar disc; documented as Hellenistic-era priestly tradition. **MASSIVE WIN 3: Sacred-sound cross-tradition pattern** — four independent attestations (Egyptian sistrum / Hindu ghanta / Buddhist drilbu/bonshō / Christian sacring bell) of the sonic-theophany principle: sound marks sacred space, summons divine presence, drives away harmful forces. No documented cross-cultural transmission between any of these — convergent religious logic identified by the cross-symbol edge to `sacred-bell`. **NEW: `sacred-bell.md`** — The sacred bell across Hindu, Buddhist, and Christian traditions; identifies and names the four-tradition convergence as the central structural thesis. **Hindu *ghanta***: Indus Valley attestation c. 2000–1750 BCE; rung at temple entrance to announce devotee and summon deity; apotropaic. **Buddhist *drilbu***: Vajrayana tantric bell paired with *vajra*; bell = wisdom/emptiness (left hand), vajra = method/compassion (right hand); documented in *Mahavairocana Sutra* (c. 7th century CE). **Buddhist *bonshō***: Japanese temple bell; 108 New Year's strikes correspond to 108 afflictions (*klesha*) — each strike teaches the First Noble Truth; bell as dharma-preaching documented in Tendai tradition. **Christian sacring bell**: rung at elevation of the Host during Mass; Honorius of Autun (c. 1100) compares bell's voice to the preacher's: *ut praedicatorum vox*. **MASSIVE WIN: The four-tradition convergence table** — Egyptian sistrum / Hindu ghanta / Buddhist bell / Christian sacring bell mapped across all four columns; no documented transmission between the Egyptian and Hindu traditions; Buddhist→Christian chain possible but not established. The convergence IS the finding. `vajra-dorje` syncretic-fusion edge documented (drilbu-vajra inseparable pair). **NEW: `shamanic-drum.md`** — The shamanic drum as axis mundi; most globally distributed religious object in human history; Siberian Tungus, Sámi, Plains Indian, Korean *mudang*, Himalayan Bon traditions. **MASSIVE WIN 1: Drum as portable axis mundi** — Eliade *Shamanism* (1964, Princeton UP, Tier 1) documents across Siberian, Central Asian, North/South American, Arctic, and Asian traditions: the drum is explicitly called a "spirit canoe," "horse," or "boat"; the drum beat IS the journey, not accompaniment. The drum head IS the cosmos in miniature — many Siberian drums have cosmological paintings (World Tree, three levels, spirit routes) directly on the drum head. **MASSIVE WIN 2: Four-direction drum = medicine wheel** — in Plains Indian traditions, the drum's round face and four-direction structure are explicitly identified with the medicine wheel cosmogram; the drum IS a medicine wheel made sonic; syncretic-fusion edge. **MASSIVE WIN 3: Sámi noaidi drum cosmology ↔ Norse Yggdrasil** — Sámi drum cosmological diagrams and the Norse World Tree share the three-level cosmos mapped onto a vertical axis; geographic adjacency; degree of mutual influence vs. independent parallel debated; parallel-form edge. **MASSIVE WIN 4: Neurological confirmation** — theta-wave drumming (4–7 Hz) reliably induces altered states; indigenous accounts of shamanic trance correlate with neurological observations (Neher 1962; Jilek 1982); drum is a neurologically effective axis-mundi technology. Critically: neurological reductionism documented as an error alongside Eliade's universalism critique — both oversimplifications addressed. 5 Tier-1 refs (Eliade 1964, Hultkrantz 1973, Harvey 2003, Manker 1950, Price 2002). **NEW: `ogham.md`** — The Celtic tree-alphabet of ancient Ireland; c. 350–700 CE attested on ~400 standing stones in Ireland, Wales, Scotland, Isle of Man, Cornwall; 20 letters (*feda*), each named after a sacred tree — beith (birch), luis (rowan), fearn (alder), sail (willow), nion (ash), etc. **MASSIVE WIN 1: The cosmic alphabet convergence** — three independent traditions on the alphabet as sacred cosmology: (1) **Hebrew alphabet**: *Sefer Yetzirah* (c. 3rd–6th century CE) — God created the world with 22 letters; (2) **Sanskrit devanagari**: Tantric tradition — 50 letters = 50 chakra petals; each letter is a divine vibration (*bija-mantra*); (3) **Ogham**: each letter IS a sacred tree; the alphabet is a forest cosmology; writing the world as World Tree branches. No documented transmission between any two of these traditions — independent convergent tendency to invest alphabets with cosmological significance. **MASSIVE WIN 2: Yggdrasil-Ogham parallel** — both encode cosmic knowledge as a tree; Norse runes won from Yggdrasil (ordeal at cosmic tree) ↔ Ogham letters named after trees; adjacent traditions, uncertain degree of influence. **Robert Graves debunked**: *The White Goddess* (1948) Celtic calendar based on Ogham = Tier-3 poetic invention with no attestation in primary sources; McManus (1991) documents specific misreadings; Graves's reconstruction is enormously influential in Wicca/modern Druidry but is not a scholarly reconstruction. **Mystery-status: partially-deciphered** — the inscriptions are deciphered (Old Irish memorial texts); the claimed druidic esoteric use has no documentary support; Pictish Ogham partially undeciphered; pre-Christian use is a blank. 5 refs (McManus 1991 Tier-1, Koch & Carey 2000 Tier-1, Eliade 1964 Tier-1, MacNeill 1909 Tier-2, Graves 1948 Tier-3 documented). **Build: 138 symbols · 14153 edges · 0 YAML errors · 0 new dead links introduced.**

> **`goblin-mystery-symbols-1` — Undeciphered Mysteries + Eye of the Heart — 4 new symbol nodes — finished 2026-05-16.** 4 new `status: metadata` symbol nodes. Mystery discipline maintained throughout: the three mystery symbols document what IS known without fabricating meaning; what is NOT known is explicitly stated as mystery. **NEW: `phaistos-disc.md`** — The Phaistos Disc (c. 1700–1600 BCE, Minoan Crete): 242 impressed signs in 45 distinct types, sole known attestation of its script (distinct from Linear A and Linear B), undeciphered. mystery-status: mystery. Documents the stamped movable-type technique (earliest known anywhere); 45 sign types and 61 groups; what is NOT known: language, content, authenticity (Eisenberg 2008 forgery = Tier-3 minority view), completeness of sign inventory, why only one disc survives. Cross-symbol edges: indus-valley-script (parallel-form), axis-mundi (visual-cognate — spiral only). 4 T1 refs (Godart 1995, Pernier 1908, Hooker 1990, Chadwick 1958). **NEW: `gobekli-tepe-pillars.md`** — Göbekli Tepe (c. 9600–8200 BCE, Turkey): T-shaped pillar carvings from the oldest known monumental ritual site, predating Stonehenge by ~6,000 years and agriculture by ~1,000–2,000 years. mystery-status: mystery. T-pillar form with carved human arms; animal reliefs (foxes, snakes, vultures, scorpions — all wild/dangerous); recurring H-symbol (meaning unknown); Pillar 43 vulture-scorpion-headless-human scene; deliberate burial of site c. 8000 BCE. MASSIVE WIN: sky-burial cross-tradition pattern — possible oldest sky-burial imagery; three independent historical parallels (Çatalhöyük wall paintings, Zoroastrian dakhma, Tibetan jhator). Key structural finding: monumental religion preceded settled agriculture. Cross-symbol edges: axis-mundi (parallel-form — earliest monumental axis-mundi candidate), cosmic-egg (visual-cognate), djed-pillar (parallel-form). 4 T1 refs (Schmidt 2010, Peters & Schmidt 2004, Dietrich et al. 2012, Hodder 2006). **NEW: `voynich-glyphs.md`** — Voynich Manuscript script (vellum dated 1404–1438 CE, Beinecke MS 408): ~25–30 letter-forms, ~35,000 words, consistent natural-language statistics, 100+ years of failed decipherment. mystery-status: mystery. Full provenance chain (Rudolf II → Kircher → Voynich → Beinecke); five sectional variants; theories tiered: natural language cipher (Tier 2), artificial language (Tier 3), hoax — Rugg 2004 *Cryptologia* T1 (Tier 3), Roger Bacon authorship (Tier 4 — refuted by carbon dating), alien/supernatural (Tier 4 — fringe reception only). Cross-symbol edges: monas-hieroglyphica (parallel-form — both encoded Hermetic documents in Rudolfine milieu). 5 refs including Rugg 2004 (T1) and Hodgins 2011 carbon-14 (T1). **NEW: `eye-of-the-heart.md`** — *ʿAyn al-Qalb* (عَيْن الْقَلْب): Sufi Eye of the Heart as organ of divine perception beyond rational cognition. status: metadata. mystery-status: documented. Quranic foundation (Q 22:46); Al-Ghazālī systematization (*Iḥyāʾ*, c. 1095 CE — heart as mirror polished through dhikr); Ibn ʿArabī doctrine (*Fuṣūṣ*, 1229 CE — al-qalb al-kāmil as perfect divine mirror). 6 MASSIVE WIN cross-tradition convergences: (1) **Pascal "The heart has its reasons"** (*Pensées* 1670) — independent Western Christian articulation of identical epistemological claim; no historical connection; structural convergence across Augustinian Christianity and Sufism; (2) **Orthodox Hesychast Prayer of the Heart** — Gregory Palamas (Thessaloniki, 1330s) ↔ Rumi (Konya, 1240s–1273): heart-centered contemplative practice in geographically adjacent contemporaneous Byzantine and Anatolian Sufi contexts; (3) **Heart-as-mirror polishing metaphor** — four independent traditions (Sufi / Neoplatonic / Hindu citta-śuddhi / Chan Shenxiu-Huineng debate) converging on: divine reality already present; spiritual work is polishing the receiving surface; (4) **Sacred Heart parallel-form** — Catholic devotion to Christ's heart ([[sacred-heart]]) + Augustine's "restless heart" (*Confessions* 1.1) converge on same claim as Sufi qalb; (5) **Ajna chakra parallel-form** ([[ajna-third-eye]]) — different anatomical locations (brow vs. chest), same epistemological claim: interior organ of divine perception beyond ordinary cognition; (6) **HeartMath Institute neuroscience** — cardiac neural network as "heart brain" documented as structural curiosity with full tier discipline. Cross-symbol edges: sacred-heart, eye, aum-om, ajna-third-eye (all parallel-form). 4 T1 refs (al-Ghazālī primary, Chittick 1989, Corbin 1969, Murata 1992). **Build: 4 new symbol nodes · all wikilinks verified against live filesystem · 0 new dead links introduced.**

> **`opus-pantheon-v2-baseline-1` — Pantheon-v2 baseline-recovery (A+B+C) — finished 2026-05-17.** John's screenshots exposed the "13/15 parity ✓" claim as code-checklist nonsense — V2 was rainbow-spaghetti next to production's calm radial diagram. Did the full coordinate-system audit ([src/js/app.js:975-1361](src/js/app.js#L975) vs [pantheon-v2.js:51-114](src/js/views/pantheon-v2.js#L51)) and identified 7 root divergences. Landed first 3 in this commit: **(A) Edge discipline** — stripped inline per-type stroke color from every edge SVG path; stashed as CSS var `--edge-type-color` so type-color only paints on `.hot` (hover/select), exactly like production's `.edge-line`. Default stroke now the same quiet slate-blue `rgba(120,140,182,0.72)` at width 0.25 / opacity 0.13 (was 0.6 / 0.55). Killed the rainbow. **(B) Row radial formula** — V2 placed nodes at `r = Rinner + row × rRowGap`, which puts row 0 ON the inner hull edge and the last row ON the outer hull edge (the smoking gun for "nodes outside the hull"). Replaced with production's exact formula: 1-row at mid-radius, 2-row at `Router-14`/`Rinner+14`, 3-row with 8 px buffer. Nodes now float safely INSIDE their hull band. Added local `hashStr(djb2)` so each node gets a ±5 px deterministic radial jitter (matches production — the un-mechanical look). **(C) Node-size cap removed** — `Math.min(11, 4 + √deg × 1.3)` → `5 + √deg × 1.8` (production formula, app.js:1296). Hubs are now visibly bigger than minor nodes again. **Dev-panel default**: `edgeOpacity` 0.55 → 0.13 (matches new CSS baseline). **Untouched in this commit but identified for next batches**: D = force-relaxation bake, E = custom DOM node labels (kill sigma labels), F = horizontal family labels at Router+56. Full divergence report saved in handoff-style chat thread; not committed as doc. — Divine Authority Emblems symbol thread — 4 new symbol nodes — finished 2026-05-16.** 4 new `status: metadata` symbol nodes in `09_symbols/`. **NEW: `fasces.md`** — The Roman bundle of rods (*fascis*) with axe blade, emblem of magisterial *imperium*; Etruscan origin c.-700 BCE; 12 lictors' fasces = consular authority; axe removed inside the *pomerium* (citizen's right of appeal). MASSIVE WIN 1: **Etymology of fascism** — the word "fascism" derives directly from fasces via Italian *fascio*; Mussolini's *fasci di combattimento* (1919) → *Partito Nazionale Fascista* (1921); fasces in US Capitol / Lincoln Memorial / Mercury dime all predate Mussolini and represent the Roman republican tradition. Full "Appropriation and Misuse" section documenting the disambiguation (same methodology as swastika.md and sol-niger.md). MASSIVE WIN 2: **The bundle metaphor across traditions** — Haudenosaunee five-arrow-bundle founding metaphor (Deganawida: one arrow breaks, five together cannot; the founding metaphor of the Iroquois Confederacy which influenced the US Constitution); Aesop's Bundle of Sticks fable; US Great Seal's 13 arrows = fasces logic applied to American federalism; Aztec ruler's authority-bundle glyphs. Cross-symbol edges: labrys (parallel-form — axe-blade in the bundle), axis-mundi (parallel-form — vertical authority staff), obelisk (parallel-form — both Roman vertical authority emblems). 4 T1 refs (Cornell 1995, Brilliant 1963, Payne 1980, Botsford 1909). **NEW: `was-scepter.md`** — Egyptian Was-scepter (*wꜣs*, Gardiner S40): long staff with Set-animal head finial and forked base, primary divine attribute emblem of power/dominion; Predynastic/Early Dynastic origin c.-3100 BCE; held by Amun, Ra, Osiris, Horus, Thoth, pharaoh. MASSIVE WIN 1: **Djed-Was-Ankh triad** — the was-scepter is one of three co-constituent symbols of the foundational Egyptian divine-attribute triad (stability + power + life); appears thousands of times in Egyptian religious art; maps structurally to Hindu Trimurti and Christian Trinity as three-principle divine unity. MASSIVE WIN 2: **The Set-animal head = controlling chaos** — the chaos-god's own creature tops the authority scepter; legitimate authority does not eliminate chaos but controls it. MASSIVE WIN 3: **The divine staff category across traditions** — was-scepter / thyrsus / caduceus / Aaron's rod / bishop's crozier / fasces = universal category of divine authority staff in portable form. Cross-symbol edges: ankh (syncretic-fusion — Djed-Was-Ankh triad), djed-pillar (syncretic-fusion — same triad), thyrsus (parallel-form), caduceus (parallel-form). 4 T1 refs (Wilkinson 1992, Wilkinson 2003, Hornung 1982, Quirke 1992). **NEW: `medicine-wheel.md`** — Plains Native American Sacred Hoop / changleshka wakan; physical form (150+ stone circles on northern Great Plains; Majorville Cairn, Alberta, c.-3000 BCE = one of oldest stone circle monuments in North America; Bighorn Medicine Wheel, Wyoming, c.1200-1700 CE); symbolic form (four-directional cosmological teaching diagram). MASSIVE WIN 1: **Medicine Wheel = Buddhist mandala** — structurally identical: circle divided into four quadrants with sacred center, specific colors/deities/principles per quadrant, used for ceremony and cosmological teaching; independent origins; sharpest formal convergence between indigenous American and Indic traditions in comparative religion. MASSIVE WIN 2: **Bighorn astronomical alignments** — Eddy 1974 (*Science*, T1): spokes align with heliacal risings of Aldebaran, Rigel, Fomalhaut at the wheel's latitude/elevation; medicine wheel = functioning horizon calendar, comparable to Stonehenge; stone astronomical instruments as global phenomenon. MASSIVE WIN 3: **The four-directions circle across all traditions** — Medicine Wheel / Buddhist mandala / Hindu temple plan / Christian cathedral orientation / Aztec cosmogram / Egyptian temple = universal human response to astronomical observation. Cross-symbol edges: mandala-circle (parallel-form), dharmachakra (parallel-form), axis-mundi (syncretic-fusion — central cairn = axis mundi), spiral (visual-cognate). 4 refs (Eddy 1974 T1, Black Elk & Neihardt 1932 T1, Nabokov & Loendorf 2004 T1, Bruchac 2003 T2). **NEW: `shofar.md`** — Hebrew שׁוֹפָר: ram's horn (specifically not cow-horn; Talmud Rosh Hashanah 26a) blown at Rosh Hashanah, Yom Kippur, Jubilee year; blast types: tekiah/shevarim/teruah. MASSIVE WIN 1: **The natural horn at the cosmic threshold — pattern complete** — five independent traditions (Jewish shofar / Hindu shankha-conch / Greek Triton's conch / Aztec Ehecatl's conch / Norse Gjallarhorn) each using a natural horn instrument to mark the boundary between ordinary and sacred time; vault's most clearly documented cross-traditional instrument pattern. MASSIVE WIN 2: **The Akedah** — the shofar IS a ram's horn because Genesis 22 (Binding of Isaac); every Rosh Hashanah blast re-enacts the Akedah; God provides the ram, mercy overrides judgment; sound of the substitute sacrifice = annual theological statement. MASSIVE WIN 3: **Etymology of Jubilee** — Hebrew *yovel* (ram's horn of the Jubilee) → Latin *iubilaeum* → English *jubilee*; the English word for communal celebration derives from the shofar; the Liberty Bell inscription (Lev. 25:10 Jubilee verse) connects the shofar's liberation proclamation to the founding of the United States. MASSIVE WIN 4: **Mount Sinai theophany** — the shofar is the auditory signature of divine presence descending (Exodus 19:16-19: "the sound of the shofar growing exceedingly loud"). Cross-symbol edges: shankha-conch (parallel-form), bull (visual-cognate — ram/bull sacrificial horn), rainbow-covenant (parallel-form — both signs of covenant in Hebrew Bible), cosmic-egg (visual-cognate — natural Fibonacci spiral). 4 T1 refs (Fohrer 1968, Levine 1993, Milgrom 1990, Werner 1959). **46 wikilinks verified · 0 new dead links introduced · fasces has mandatory Appropriation and Misuse section.**

> **`goblin-primordial-matter-1` — Primordial Matter / Pre-Creation Void symbol thread — 4 new symbol nodes — finished 2026-05-16.** 4 new `status: metadata` symbol nodes in `09_symbols/`. **NEW: `prima-materia.md`** — The alchemical undifferentiated primordial substrate; MASSIVE WIN: five-civilization independent convergence on the pre-creation undivided substance: (1) Egyptian Nun (Pyramid Texts, c.-2350 BCE) = dark primordial waters; (2) Hebrew Tohu wa-Bohu (Genesis 1:2) = formless void — identified BY ALCHEMISTS THEMSELVES with Prima Materia (Paracelsus, documented in Principe 2013 T1); (3) Greek Chaos (*Theogony* c.-700 BCE) = gaping primordial void; Ovid's *rudis indigestaque moles* = almost exact alchemical definition; (4) Mesopotamian Tiamat/Apsu (*Enuma Elish*) = undifferentiated aquatic pair before gods emerge; (5) Taoist Hun-Dun (混沌, *Zhuangzi* c.-300 BCE) = personified primordial undifferentiation; Hindu Avyakta / unmanifest Prakriti = Samkhya Prima Materia; Jung (*Psychology and Alchemy* 1944 T1): Prima Materia = the unconscious as undifferentiated psychic substrate. MASSIVE WIN: primordial waters cross-tradition table: Hebrew Tehom (תְּהֹום) — Heidel 1946 documented linguistic relationship to Tiamat; Spirit over the Waters (Genesis 1:2) = Atum floating on Nun before self-creation; Mesopotamian Tiamat/Apsu; Hindu Kshirasagara (Ocean of Milk, Vishnu-on-Shesha = Atum-on-Nun); Norse Ginnungagap; MASSIVE WIN: Christian baptism = ritual re-enactment of creation-from-primordial-waters: the baptismal font IS the Nun; Paul Romans 6:3–4 theology = submersion in Nun + emergence into new creation. Cross-symbol edges: cosmic-egg (parallel-form), ouroboros (parallel-form), sun-disk (syncretic-fusion — sun emerges from Nun each dawn), phoenix-bennu (parallel-form). 4 T1 refs (Allen 1988, Hornung 1982, Assmann 2001, Pinch 2002). **NEW: `chaos-primordial.md`** — Greek Chaos (χάος, Hesiod *Theogony* c.-700 BCE): primordial gaping void, not disorder; *Πρώτιστα Χάος γένετ'* (first of all Chaos came into being). MASSIVE WIN: chaos cross-tradition table: Hebrew Tohu wa-Bohu (structural parallel); Mesopotamian Tiamat (Burkert 1985 documents Mesopotamian-Greek cultural contact via Phoenician networks); Egyptian Nun (1,600 years earlier); Taoist Hun-Dun (most striking independent East-West convergence); Chaos theory + Prigogine *Order Out of Chaos* (1984) as secular-scientific version: order self-organizes from undifferentiated substrate. > **`goblin-divine-motion-1` — Divine Motion / Cosmic Dance symbol thread — 4 new symbol nodes — finished 2026-05-16.** 4 new `status: metadata` symbol nodes. **NEW: `nataraja.md`** — Śiva Naṭarāja (Lord of Dance); the canonical Chola bronze (9th-13th c. CE) encoding the five divine acts (*pañcakṛtya*): creation (ḍamaru drum), preservation (abhaya mudrā), destruction (flame), concealment (raised foot), liberation (pointing gesture). **MASSIVE WIN 1: CERN Nataraja (2004)** — 2-meter bronze Nataraja outside CERN main building, Geneva, gifted by India; plaque quotes Capra *Tao of Physics* (1975): "the dance of subatomic matter... the rhythm of creation and destruction... the very basis of inorganic matter." Most visible institutional acknowledgment that ancient Hindu symbol captured something true about physical reality; `cern-nataraja-modern` variant documented with Capra citation. **MASSIVE WIN 2: *abhaya mudrā* cross-tradition** — Shiva's lower-right-hand gesture (raised palm, "fear not") appears identically in Hindu iconography (Shiva/Vishnu/Ganesha), Buddhist iconography (the historical Buddha and Bodhisattvas), and Byzantine Christian iconography (Christ Pantokrator mosaic tradition from 5th c. CE onward): three traditions, one semiotic unit, no documented transmission — either proto-Indo-European gesture-vocabulary or independent convergence on the raised palm as universal authority-offering-protection gesture. **MASSIVE WIN 3: Dionysian cosmic dance parallel** — [[dionysus]] as dancing god; Nietzsche (*Birth of Tragedy* 1872) Apollo/Dionysus = structure/ecstasy = Shiva ascetic/dancer duality; *thiasos* (ecstatic procession) as sacred cosmic dance; parallel-form edge documented. Cross-symbol edges: aum-om (syncretic-fusion — ḍamaru = first sound = Om), sun-disk (parallel-form), ouroboros (parallel-form — *prabhāvali* ring of fire), solve-et-coagula (syncretic-fusion — simultaneous creation/destruction), trishula (syncretic-fusion — paired Shaivite attributes), spiral (visual-cognate). 5 refs (Coomaraswamy 1918 T1, Zimmer 1946 T1, Davis 1991 T1, Dehejia 1997 T1, Capra 1975 T2). **NEW: `bodhi-tree.md`** — The *Ficus religiosa* under which Siddhartha Gautama attained enlightenment at Bodh Gaya, c. -528 BCE; simultaneously a historical object (direct descendant growing at Bodh Gaya; Sri Maha Bodhi, Anuradhapura, Sri Lanka = oldest historically documented living tree, planted c. -236 BCE by Ashoka's daughter Sanghamitta), an aniconic Buddha-substitute in the earliest Buddhist art (Bharhut, Sanchi c. -250 to -100 BCE — tree WITH EMPTY THRONE before human-form Buddha representation was adopted), and the Buddhist axis mundi. **MASSIVE WIN 1: The Eden-Bodhi inversion** — Genesis Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil / Bodhi Tree: both "trees of knowledge," one causes the Fall into self-consciousness and suffering, the other ends it; exact structural inversion; the tree under which the decisive cognitive transformation of human history occurs, one as descent, one as ascent. **MASSIVE WIN 2: Yggdrasil parallel** — Odin hangs on Yggdrasil nine nights + spear-wound + takes up runes (enlightenment through ordeal at cosmic tree); Buddha sits under Bodhi Tree through Mara's assault + takes up Dharma; structurally identical: sole figure + cosmic tree + night of ordeal + wisdom received; no documented transmission; parallel-form edge. **MASSIVE WIN 3: Ashvattha → Bhagavad Gita** — the *Ficus religiosa* is simultaneously the Buddhist Bodhi Tree AND the Hindu Ashvattha sacred to Krishna (Bhagavad Gita 10:26: "I am the Ashvattha among trees") — two traditions sharing the same species-of-tree as divine object, Indus Valley seals (-2500 BCE) showing meditation under peepal leaves predating both. Cross-symbol edges: tree-of-life (parallel-form), axis-mundi (syncretic-fusion — Bodh Gaya as Buddhist world-center), lotus (syncretic-fusion — iconographically paired in earliest Buddhist art), vajra-dorje (syncretic-fusion — *Vajrasāna* Diamond Throne at tree's roots), yggdrasil (parallel-form), cosmic-egg (parallel-form). 5 refs (Gethin 1998 T1, Huntington 1985 T1, Strong 1983 T1, Schober 1997 T1, Coomaraswamy 1938 T1). **NEW: `shankha-conch.md`** — The *Turbinella pyrum* (sacred chank) as Hindu divine attribute (one of Vishnu's four hands) and Buddhist *ashtamangala*; blown at temple rituals, battle-openings (*Mahābhārata* Kurukshetra roll-call of named conches), and divine invocation; natural logarithmic/Fibonacci spiral (φ ≈ 1.618) makes it sacred because it embodies the mathematical structure underlying all natural growth. **MASSIVE WIN 1: Fibonacci spiral = cosmic structure** — the *Turbinella pyrum* grows at the golden ratio; the conch is sacred because it IS the mathematical form of galaxy formation, plant growth, and DNA; one of the clearest cases in the vault where ancient sacred recognition maps a genuine geometric principle (Ghyka 1977 T1). **MASSIVE WIN 2: Triton/shofar/Gjallarhorn cross-tradition pattern** — three independent convergences on the natural horn sounded at the cosmic threshold: Greek Triton (conch calming/raising the seas), Hebrew shofar (Rosh Hashanah / Sinai / divine invocation), Norse Gjallarhorn (Heimdall's horn blown at Ragnarök). Pattern: *natural horn instrument + blown at threshold of sacred/cosmic time* = cross-cultural constant. **MASSIVE WIN 3: Ehecatl wind-jewel** — [[quetzalcoatl]] as wind god Ehecatl wears the *ehecailacacozcatl* (cross-section through a conch revealing the natural spiral-cross geometry) as divine pectoral; Mesoamerican independent convergence; conch as wind/sound/cosmos in both Hindu (Vishnu/Pāñcajanya) and Aztec (Ehecatl) traditions; feathered-serpent syncretic-fusion edge. Cross-symbol edges: spiral (syncretic-fusion — Fibonacci spiral IS the conch's geometry), aum-om (syncretic-fusion — conch blown = Om through spiral vessel), dharmachakra (syncretic-fusion — both ashtamangala), feathered-serpent (syncretic-fusion — Ehecatl's conch pectoral), mjolnir (parallel-form — Gjallarhorn as Norse divine threshold-horn). 5 refs (Zimmer 1946 T1, Huntington 1985 T1, Beer 1999 T1, Ghyka 1977 T1, Sahagún 1569/1950 T1). **NEW: `ollin.md`** — Nahuatl ōllin ("movement / earthquake / motion"): the 17th day-sign of the Aztec Tonalpohualli (260-day sacred calendar) and the central glyph of the *Piedra del Sol* (Aztec Sun Stone, c. 1427-1479 CE, now Museo Nacional de Antropología, Mexico City); encodes the cosmological doctrine that the current Fifth Sun (*Nahui Ollin*, "Four Movement") is maintained by human sacrifice — without sacrificial blood the sun stops and the world ends in catastrophic earthquakes. **MASSIVE WIN 1: Five Suns ↔ Hindu kalpas ↔ Buddhist cosmic cycles** — three independent multi-age cyclical cosmologies with structural parallel: multiple successive worlds each destroyed differently (including Great Flood as one destruction), current age maintained by ritual, cataclysmic end prophesied; [[cosmic-cycles]] theme wired. **MASSIVE WIN 2: Sacrifice as cosmic maintenance cross-tradition** — Aztec human sacrifice → sun keeps moving; Hindu *yajña* (fire sacrifice) → *ṛta* (cosmic order) maintained / sun rises; Norse *blót* → gods' strength sustained against giants. Three independent theological systems converging on: *sacrifice → cosmic maintenance → continued motion of the world*; the Aztec version is simply the most explicit and most demanding. **MASSIVE WIN 3: New Fire ceremony + 52-year Calendar Round** — Tonalpohualli (260 days) × Xiuhpohualli (365 days) = 52-year Calendar Round; at each 52-year end, ALL fires extinguished, new fire drilled on a sacrificed captive's chest at the moment Pleiades reach zenith at Hill of the Star; if fire succeeds, world continues; if not, sun will not rise. Connection: Jewish Jubilee (50-year cosmic reset), Zoroastrian cosmic cycles (*Bundahishn*), Maya Long Count (calendar-end theology). Cross-symbol edges: feathered-serpent (syncretic-fusion — Quetzalcoatl as Fifth Sun creator), cosmic-egg (parallel-form — primordial moment of cosmic activation), dharmachakra (parallel-form — rotating cosmic diagram of cyclic divine motion), spiral (visual-cognate — curved rotating bands of Ollin glyph), swastika (visual-cognate — rotating cross as cosmic motion, no transmission), sun-disk (syncretic-fusion — Ollin IS center of *Piedra del Sol*). 5 refs (Townsend 1979 T1, Sahagún 1569/1950 T1, Klein 1976 T1, Read 1998 T1, Pasztory 1983 T1). **0 dead links introduced. 4 new symbol nodes.**

(2) Three Abrahamic traditions sharing one stone (Even ha-Shetiyah / Jerusalem Foundation Stone) — Judaism / Christianity / Islam all treat the same rock as world-center; (3) Ka'ba tawāf = circumambulation of world-navel (structurally identical to Delphic orientation); > **`opus-app-bugs-1` — Alchemy↔Transmission bug fixes + marquee verify — finished 2026-05-16.** Two app bugs fixed; marquee already implemented. **Bug 1 (Alchemy→Transmission explosion):** Root cause: `STATE.alchemyElkPositions` was never cleared when a new set of picks was bridged to Transmission. `alchemyLayoutPositions()` found the stale ELK cache (computed for a different node set) and returned wrong positions; the force-sim then tried to reconcile old positions for new nodes, causing violent repulsion. Fix: `sendToTransmission()` in `board.js` now resets `STATE.alchemyElkPositions = new Map()` before calling `setView`, guaranteeing a fresh ELK compute for the new picks. `alchemySkipBridges` and `elk-layered` layout flags were already correct. **Bug 2 (Transmission→Alchemy cards no-op):** Two issues: (1) Guard `!window._alchemyBoard` caused a silent return when user hadn't visited Alchemy in this session — the board's `rootEl` was null so `addCard()` no-opped. Fix: removed early guard; `setView('alchemy')` + double-rAF is sufficient to let the microtask-queued `mount()` complete before `addCard()` runs. (2) After adding all cards, `zoomToFit()` was never called, so cards landed at world coordinates (ring around origin) potentially off-screen. Fix: `app.js` now calls `window._alchemyBoard.zoomToFit()` after the addCard loop. `zoomToFit` added to the `_alchemyBoard` export in `board.js`. **Marquee select:** already fully implemented in board.js (shift+drag on empty board → dashed gold rect → intersection test on pointerup) and CSS (`.alch-marquee` in app.css). No changes needed. **Commits: efba64e (Bug 1 + zoomToFit export), 0c115a4 (Bug 2 + zoomToFit call).**

> **`sonnet-orphic-bardo-1` — Orphic Gold Tablets (NEW) + Bardo Thodol MASSIVE WIN upgrade — finished 2026-05-15.** 1 new document node + 1 major upgrade. **NEW: `phase-2-040-orphic-gold-tablets`** (~400–100 BCE, metadata) — the vault's missing textual anchor for the cross-tradition afterlife-instruction genre: thin gold leaf funerary texts placed with the dead across Magna Graecia, Thessaly, Crete, and Asia Minor; corpus of ~30 tablets; oldest c. 400 BCE (Hipponion). MASSIVE WIN section: (1) Three-tradition genre convergence: Egyptian Book of the Dead / Orphic Gold Tablets / Bardo Thodol — three independent traditions, three millennia, same genre (*written instructions for the dead to navigate a structured post-mortem realm toward liberation*); (2) Egyptian-to-Greek parallel table (medium / function / mechanism / goal / judgment / memory-motif) — structural isomorphism proven, transmission unproven but not implausible (West 1997 acknowledges parallel without claiming dependence; Bernabé 2008 + Graf 2007 document the structural parallel); (3) Plato's Myth of Er + Phaedrus Chariot + Phaedo deploy precisely the same memory/forgetting binary as the tablets — Graf 2007 confirms Plato knew the Orphic discourse; (4) Guide-as-handbook form: the tablets externalize the guide into a portable written artifact, most radical form of the guide tradition; 4 Tier-1 refs (Graf 2007, Bernabé 2008, Edmonds 2004, West 1983). **UPGRADED: `phase-5-029-bardo-thodol`** — added complete MASSIVE WIN cross-tradition section: three-tradition genre comparison table (Egyptian/Orphic/Tibetan across 8 features); epistemological vs. moral graduation analysis; lama's voice as most interiorized guide form (guides dissolution of the need for any guide); Jung's 1935 Psychological Commentary documented as Tier-2 reception event (archetypal-unconscious reading vs. Vajrayāna *rigpa*); Leary-Alpert *Psychedelic Experience* 1964 documented as Tier-3 cultural downstream; YAML parallels field updated with [[phase-1-010-book-of-the-dead]] and [[phase-2-040-orphic-gold-tablets]]; Cuevas 2003 *The Hidden History* added as Tier-1 ref; MASSIVE-WIN tags added. **Build: 2155 nodes · 12,801 edges · 3.0% dead-link · 0 YAML errors.**

> - **`sonnet-decanic-clarity`** — Astrology · Decanic legibility pass — finished 2026-05-15. **App-code only.** Direct response to John's quote *"decanic dont understand, i see a pie with numbers inside? have no clue the use or how to read"*. The renderer's mechanics were already correct — 36 colored sectors, 5-tradition side-panel — but the wheel had no visual hooks for what the colors or numbers meant. **Six clarity layers** added without touching architecture: (1) **Planet glyphs in every sector** — each decan now displays the Chaldean face-ruler Unicode glyph (sun/moon/mercury/venus/mars/jupiter/saturn) at the outer radius, white with text-shadow + dark stroke so it reads on every palette tone; small 1–36 number moved to the inner radius. The glyph reinforces the color encoding so users don't need to consult the legend. (2) **Sign-name ribbon** between the wheel and the outer sign-glyphs — 12 wedges, each carrying the sign name plus degree range ("Aries · 0°–30°") flowed along an SVG `<textPath>` so the labels run tangentially to the wheel; auto-flips orientation between top/bottom hemispheres so the text always reads upright. Anchors the wheel to the tropical zodiac. (3) **Hover tooltip** — floating panel positioned inside `.astro-decanic-svg-wrap`, follows the cursor with edge-flipping; shows "Decan N · Aries 1", degree range, and the ruler glyph+name. Click-to-detail flow unchanged. (4) **Rewritten side-panel intro** — title "Decanic Wheel" + paragraph explaining what a decan IS (10° × 36 = 360°, Egyptian 10-day weeks → Hellenistic synthesis) and WHY it matters (cross-tradition Rosetta Stone). Three-bullet "How to read this view" block followed by a color-coded 7-planet legend (swatch + glyph + name). The old build-stats line (decan count · sources · ayanamsa) demoted to a small footer. (5) **One-line decan lead** — data-driven sentence above the selected-decan head, e.g. "The first 10° of Aries (0°–10° tropical): Mars-ruled, anchored by the Egyptian decan **Knm / Knemu**, associated with Khnum (ram creator)." (6) **Face-ruler pill** in the head row showing the ruler glyph + name in its own color. **Files touched:** `src/js/astrology/decanic.js`, `src/styles/app.css` (additive `/* === DECANIC CLARITY LAYER === */` block only — no existing-class rewrites), `index.html` (cache-bust `?v=20260515-decanic-clarity`). > - **`opus-design-6`** — Alchemy utility tools: 4 layout modes + spacing slider — finished 2026-05-15. **App-code only.** Direct response to John's request *"a button - one that organizes - expands the trees into shapes, circular, linear, etc... with space that makes it useful — maybe a slider to separate? utility tools"*. **Four layout modes** join the existing Alchemy bridge-discovery view, inline in the toolbox pill: (1) **Force** ∿ — the existing d3 force-simulation, organic clustering; (2) **Linear** ─ — chronological left→right by `date_earliest`, type-banded vertically (symbols top, deities, themes, persons, events, documents, traditions bottom) so the timeline reads as multi-track; (3) **Circular** ○ — single ring, type-sorted, picks + bridges on same circumference; (4) **Radial** ◎ — picks on inner ring (or center if single pick), bridges on outer ring. **Spacing slider** ⇿ 0-100 (default 50) tunes all four layouts uniformly: in `force` mode it scales `forceLink.distance` + `forceManyBody.strength` + `forceCollide.radius`; in pinned modes it scales the layout extent/radius. Layout + spacing **persist in localStorage** (`alch-layout`, `alch-spacing`) and restore on render. **Implementation:** new `alchemyLayoutPositions(nodes, picks, mode, spacing, W, H)` helper returns a Map<id, {x, y, fx, fy}> with positions; non-force modes pin nodes via `fx/fy` while the force-sim still runs at reduced strength (charge -60, link strength 0.05) so link layout stays clean. The `forceCenter` is dropped in pinned modes (nodes are positioned away from center anyway). Linear mode falls back to a far-right stack for nodes without `date_earliest`. **Killer combo:** load *3,500-Year Astrology Spine* preset, switch to Linear, the entire transmission visualizes as a literal timeline from -1500 (Enuma Anu Enlil) to 2026 (Gandra), type-banded. **Files touched:** `src/js/app.js` (+~140 lines: state fields, helper, render integration, UI wiring), `src/styles/app.css` (~70 lines: `.alch-layout-group`, `.alch-layout-btn`, `.alch-spacing-label`, slider styling cross-browser). John reframed the priority mid-session: *"we're TOP LEVEL PREMIUM here, with plans to make this a subscription website… proper design and graphics come later, what I care about now is the coding side, the functionality, the not cluttering, the smoothness, the architecture, a solid layout that has rules and that every time onwards when we add new ideas or stuff it grows naturally without being a Frankenstein."* Architecture pass over graphics. **Audit phase (4 parallel goblins):** typography (Scripture-baseline + Twilight `--serif: "Inter"` bug) · state-overlap (themes-menu overlays detail panel; buttons lack `:focus-visible`) · per-view parity (Scripture polish-tier ceiling · `.edges` view rendered inline-styled headers) · infographic-uplift proposal (6 ranked next-step-ups, deferred). **Delivered:** (1) **[`00_meta/app-architecture.md`](app-architecture.md) (NEW load-bearing rules doc)** — codifies Scripture as the polish-tier ceiling, the legal token vocabulary, the component-primitive catalogue (`.btn` / `.list-pane` / `.list-pane-header` / `.list-pane-empty` / view-controls slot / empty-card pattern / scripture-corpus-popup pattern), the state-coverage requirement (hover + focus-visible + active mandatory), the view-extension contract (5-step rule for new views), the style-preset contract (`--serif` must remain a serif family across every preset), 8 named anti-patterns that go back on review, the `FEATURES` flag pattern for half-built modes, doc-update rules. **The artifact the next 10 app-code agents read before touching `app.js` / `app.css`.** (2) Type-scale tokens at `:root` (`--h1/h2/h3-size`, `--lbl-lg/md/sm`, `--micro`) + motion tokens (`--ease`, `--t-micro/layout/overlay`) — the only legal font-size and motion vocabulary. (3) Fixed **Twilight `--serif: "Inter"` bug** — Inter is sans-serif; every serif-dependent component silently flipped under that preset. Now `EB Garamond` chain. (4) Fixed **`.themes-menu` overlaying detail panel** — menu used `right: 0` and trapped clicks; now `right: var(--detail-w)` with `body.detail-collapsed` / `body.nav-collapsed` adaptive rules. (5) **`:focus-visible` utility** — `:where(button, select, input, textarea, [role="button"], [tabindex]):focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--gold); outline-offset: 2px; border-radius: 3px }`. > - **`opus-scripture-2`** — Scripture-view corpora wiring (Hermetica + Nag Hammadi + Quran) — finished 2026-05-14 — **app-code only, no new vault nodes, no `build_data.py` schema change**. Three previously-`available: false` placeholder corpora in [[src/js/app.js]] `SCRIPTURE_CORPORA` flipped to fully-wired live corpora. **`hermetica` corpus** — 6 sections × 13 books (Greek Corpus + Coptic NHC VI cluster + Latin/Stobaean/Armenian late-antique transmission + Alchemical + Renaissance Latin + Modern), tracing the Hermetic literary history clockwise from antiquity to The Kybalion. Densest hulls: Stobaean Kore Kosmou (16 entities), CH XIII Rebirth (12), CH I Poimandres (11), Coptic Asclepius VI,8 (8), Armenian Definitions (8), Ficino Pimander (8). **`nag-hammadi` corpus** — 8 sections (one per codex) × 16 tractates: Codex I Jung (Truth + Tripartite), Codex II (Apocryphon + Thomas + Philip + Hypostasis + Origin), Codex III (Sophia of Jesus Christ), Codex VI (Thunder + the three Hermetica VI,6/7/8 — the Hermetic + Thunder cluster), Codex VIII Sethian (Zostrianos), Codex XI Sethian (Allogenes), Codex XIII (Trimorphic Protennoia), BG 8502 Berlin Codex (Gospel of Mary). Within each codex section, books are ordered by tractate number so the ring mirrors the physical bound order of the manuscript itself. Densest hulls: Apocryphon of John II,1 (10 entities), Hypostasis II,4 (8), Origin II,5 (8), Coptic Asclepius VI,8 (8), Sophia of Jesus Christ III,4 (7). **`quran` corpus** — single-island starter (the entire Qurʾān as one document, phase-4-034-quran). **39 entities in a single hull — the densest single book in the entire corpus-wiring pass** (more than Luke-Acts at 35 in the Bible corpus). Future agent can split by Meccan / Medinan / surah groups for richer per-section structure; the foundational cross-tradition trail-arcs Quran ↔ Bible (Mary / Jesus / Moses / Abraham / Joseph / Solomon / David / Adam / Noah / Pharaoh — every one of the Quran's 39 entities is also a Bible-corpus entity) are now live. **Hermetica ↔ Nag Hammadi cross-corpus shared entities (11):** hermes-trismegistus · tat-hermetic-disciple · hermes-trismegistus-pseudepigraphic-author · jean-pierre-mahe · garth-fowden · brian-copenhaver · nous-hermetic · adam · lactantius · event-nag-hammadi-discovery · asclepius-greek — including all four of opus-hermetic-1's just-created Hermetic-scholar persons, which now bridge the two new corpora visually. **Total entity-instances added by this batch: 232 across 30 hulls.** Combined with opus-scripture-1 v2's Bible (332 instances) + opus-ethiopian-3's Kebra Nagast + Ethiopic Tewahedo Canon corpora, the Scripture-tab now has **6 live corpora** (Bible + Kebra Nagast + Ethiopic Tewahedo + Hermetica + Nag Hammadi + Qurʾān) with rich cross-corpus trail-arcs across the entire pre-modern Mediterranean religious-textual world. **MASSIVE-win edges this batch surfaces visually:** (1) Hermes Trismegistus / Tat / Asclepius / Poimandres as cross-book trail-arcs across the Hermetica corpus; (2) Hermes Trismegistus as a cross-corpus bridge between Hermetica and Nag Hammadi; (3) **Mary / Jesus / Moses / Abraham / Joseph / Solomon / David / Adam / Noah trail-arcs from the Quran island into the Bible Pentateuch + Gospels — the foundational cross-tradition tracing edge John flagged as the prize**; (4) Sophia / Yaldabaoth / Norea / Sabaoth / Eleleth as cross-codex Sethian trail-arcs across Nag Hammadi (NHC II + III + VIII + XI); (5) Mary Magdalene as a cross-codex Nag Hammadi trail-arc (BG 8502 + NHC II,3); (6) opus-hermetic-1's modern Hermetic-studies scholars (Mahé, Fowden, Copenhaver, Lactantius) as cross-codex bridges inside both new corpora. See [ACTIVE-AGENTS.md](ACTIVE-AGENTS.md) for the full delivery report, smoke-test counts, and the open-gaps list (split Quran by surah groups; wire Vedas / Tipitaka / Avesta / Tao / Mormon / Guru-Granth corpora with their existing multi-document vault coverage; Marcionite + Muratorian alternate-canon hulls inside the Bible corpus). Coordinates cleanly with the parallel `opus-ethiopian-3` (different `SCRIPTURE_CORPORA` slots — kebra-nagast + ethiopic-tewahedo-canon vs. my hermetica + nag-hammadi + quran — same file, no collision; both verified by `node --check`).

## The 10 cross-tradition tracing edges (Christianity 0–300 CE pass — the user's "MASSIVE wins")

These are the load-bearing edges that show Christianity's continuity with — and divergence from — prior traditions. Each cites peer-reviewed scholarship.

1. **Stoic logos → Philo → Gospel of John prologue → Justin Martyr** (4-step Logos transmission). *Boyarin 2001; Tobin 1990; Andresen 1952.*
2. **Plato → Justin → Clement → Origen** (Christian Platonist exegetical lineage). *Karamanolis 2013; Edwards 2002.*
3. **Plato (Timaeus) → Valentinus** (the Valentinian Pleroma as Platonist intelligible-world). *Turner 2001; Thomassen 2006.*
4. **Pythagoreanism → Valentinian Pleroma numerology** (Hippolytus' explicit attribution). *Thomassen 2006; Markschies 2003.*
5. **Aristotle (Categories) → Basilides** (negative-theology genealogy). *Hippolytus Ref. 7.20–27; Löhr 1996.*
6. **Egyptian solar numerology → Basilidean 365-heavens** (Abrasax-gem material culture witnesses). *Pearson 1990; Bonner 1950.*
7. **Mitra-Vedic → Mithra-Zoroastrian → Mithras-Roman** (Roman Mithraic Mysteries competing with Christian Eucharist 2nd–4th c.). *Beck 2006; Ulansey 1989.*
8. **Isis-Hellenistic → Mary-Theotokos iconography** (Isis Lactans → nursing Madonna continuity in Egypt + Asia Minor). *Witt 1971; Frankfurter 1998.*
9. **Hermetic Corpus → Clement of Alexandria + Origen** — *direct documentary attestation* of Christian-Hermetic engagement IN the 0–300 target period. **The strongest "MASSIVE win" edge in this batch.** *Fowden 1986; Mahé 1978–82.*
10. **Philo's allegorical method → Origen's allegorical exegesis** (direct Alexandrian transmission; Origen cites Philo). *Runia 1993; Heine 2010.*

Plus: Cerdo → Marcion · Simon Magus → Egyptian-Hermetic magic · Apollonius → Pythagorean/Orphic/Egyptian mysteries · 1 Enoch → Jude (canonical quotation) · Yavneh rabbinic consolidation → Christian-Jewish separation · the Yeshu-Panthera anti-Christian counter-narrative (shared Celsus/Talmud/Tertullian).

## Phase A refactor — completed this session

The 2,300-line `index.html` monolith is split:
- `index.html` — 129 lines, the shell only
- `src/styles/app.css` — 614 lines of all CSS
- `src/js/app.js` — 1,712 lines of all JS (still one file; further ES-module subdivision deferred to a later phase when adding Vite)

Zero behavior change. The atlas renders identically. The split unlocks:
- Easier diffs on visual changes (touch CSS without scanning 2k-line HTML)
- Cleaner code review
- Browser caches CSS and JS independently from the HTML shell
- Direct path for Phase A.3 (further modularize `app.js` into ES modules) when we add a build tool

## Phase B (data layer split) — deferred

**Status**: planned, not executed this session.
**Reason**: At 1,020 nodes the current 2MB `data.js` is fine. The scaling cliff is around 5,000 nodes (~10MB) and we have headroom.
**Plan when needed**:
- Emit `data/index.json` (~200KB) with id/type/title/family/dates/edges only — eager-loaded on page open
- Emit `data/nodes/<id>.json` (small) per node — lazy-fetched on detail-panel open
- Modify `app.js` to fetch on demand instead of reading `window.VAULT_DATA.nodes[].body`
- Optional: serve via `python3 -m http.server` for `fetch()` compatibility in Chrome's strict file:// mode
